class="bg-body text-body" data-bs-theme="light"><div class="header text-center position-relative pt-50 pb-50"><div class="container"><div class="row text-center"><div class="col-12" style="max-width:1076px;width:100%;margin-inline:auto"><h2 class="header-heading h3 mb-3">Product Overview</h2><div class="header-description text-body mb-0"><p class="mx-auto">Learn how MetaDefender NDR and InSights deliver real-time network visibility, anomaly detection, and threat correlation<br class="d-none d-lg-block"/>across IT and OT environments to detect attacks earlier and reduce dwell time.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="video text-center position-relative pt-0 pb-50"><div class="container"><div class="row justify-content-center"><div class="col-12 col-lg-12 col-xl-11 col-xxl-10 text-body mx-auto"><div class="wistia overflow-hidden rounded-4"><div class="wistia_placeholder wistia_async_1l6p4mmz5r seo=false controlsVisibleOnLoad=false videoFoam=true" style="aspect-ratio:16 / 9"></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section id="features" class="bg-body text-body" data-bs-theme="light" style="background:url(&quot;https://static.opswat.com/cms/images/ndr-lod-aurora.svg&quot;) center / cover no-repeat"><div class="header text-center position-relative pt-regular pb-0"><div class="container"><div class="row text-center"><div class="col-12" style="max-width:1076px;width:100%;margin-inline:auto"><h2 class="header-heading h3 mb-0">Redefine Your Network Hunting Capabilities</h2></div></div></div></div><div class="cards text-left position-relative pt-50 pb-50"><div class="container"><div class="row row-cols-1 row-cols-md-2 row-cols-lg-3 row-gap-4 text-start"><div class="col" data-aos="fade-up" data-aos-delay="0"><div class="card border-0 card-light bg-white"><div class="card-body py-4"><div class="icon-network-connect-safe mb-4 fs-42 icon-gradient text-primary lh-1" aria-hidden="true"></div><h3 class="text-sh2 fw-medium line-clamp-3 mb-18">Encrypted Session Analysis</h3><div class="text-body description"><div class="payload-richtext prose max-w-none"><p>Encrypted session analysis is performed against SSL/TLS connections as well as encrypted session attributes/characteristics to identify malicious activity as well as command and control activity even without inline decryption in place.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="col" data-aos="fade-up" data-aos-delay="200"><div class="card border-0 card-light bg-white"><div class="card-body py-4"><div class="icon-benefits-fast mb-4 fs-42 icon-gradient text-primary lh-1" aria-hidden="true"></div><h3 class="text-sh2 fw-medium line-clamp-3 mb-18">High-Performance Inspection</h3><div class="text-body description"><div class="payload-richtext prose max-w-none"><p>Swiftly detect and respond to unusual and suspicious behavior. MetaDefender NDR enables the proactive identification of potential threats at network throughput speeds of up to 40Gb per second.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="col" data-aos="fade-up" data-aos-delay="400"><div class="card border-0 card-light bg-white"><div class="card-body py-4"><div class="icon-threats-known mb-4 fs-42 icon-gradient text-primary lh-1" aria-hidden="true"></div><h3 class="text-sh2 fw-medium line-clamp-3 mb-18">Empower Threat Hunters</h3><div class="text-body description"><div class="payload-richtext prose max-w-none"><p>Streamline investigative workflows for your SOC team with our integrated incident response, intrusion analysis, remediation, event triage, and breach containment capabilities.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="col" data-aos="fade-up" data-aos-delay="600"><div class="card border-0 card-light bg-white"><div class="card-body py-4"><div class="icon-threats-intrusion mb-4 fs-42 icon-gradient text-primary lh-1" aria-hidden="true"></div><h3 class="text-sh2 fw-medium line-clamp-3 mb-18">Breach Detection &amp; Containment</h3><div class="text-body description"><div class="payload-richtext prose max-w-none"><p>Breach detection analysis is performed on every network connection attempt, established connection, and domain resolution attempt leveraging a compilation of applied Threat Intelligence from MetaDefender InSights as well as advanced heuristics and analytics crafted by our threat analysts.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="col" data-aos="fade-up" data-aos-delay="800"><div class="card border-0 card-light bg-white"><div class="card-body py-4"><div class="icon-technologies-dlp mb-4 fs-42 icon-gradient text-primary lh-1" aria-hidden="true"></div><h3 class="text-sh2 fw-medium line-clamp-3 mb-18">Data Loss Prevention (DLP)</h3><div class="text-body description"><div class="payload-richtext prose max-w-none"><p>Safeguard your vital data with our Data Loss Prevention capabilities. With advanced context and content inspection of carved files, you’ll be able to detect and prevent data exfiltration, ensuring the protection of PII, PHI, sensitive, proprietary, and even user-defined information within your environment.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="col" data-aos="fade-up" data-aos-delay="1000"><div class="card border-0 card-light bg-white"><div class="card-body py-4"><div class="icon-process-workflow mb-4 fs-42 icon-gradient text-primary lh-1" aria-hidden="true"></div><h3 class="text-sh2 fw-medium line-clamp-3 mb-18">Go Deeper and Expose More</h3><div class="text-body description"><div class="payload-richtext prose max-w-none"><p>Go beyond Layer 7 of the OSI model with Deep File Inspection® (DFI) to process content embedded within the original files extracted from the network traffic. These inspection operations typically result with an increase in the detection space of 4x for higher fidelity detections and less evasions an attacker can leverage.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section class="bg-body text-body" data-bs-theme="light"><div class="text-left position-relative product-docs pt-regular
